# Fitur C1–C9: token, drag+snap, HP, ukuran


--- FILE: client\src\features\token\AddTokenSheet.tsx ---
import { useState } from 'react';
import { TOKEN_COLORS } from '@myvtt/shared';
import BottomSheet from '../../components/BottomSheet';
import { useRoomStore } from '../room/store';

interface AddTokenSheetProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const inputClass =
  'min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-3 text-base text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:outline-none';

/**
 * Bottom sheet tambah token (C1): nama + warna preset + pratinjau inisial.
 * Hanya dibuka DM; posisi spawn dihitung server (tidak dikirim klien).
 */
export default function AddTokenSheet({ open, onClose }: AddTokenSheetProps) {
  const addToken = useRoomStore((state) => state.addToken);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [color, setColor] = useState<string>(TOKEN_COLORS[4]);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const initial = name.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase();

  async function handleAdd() {
    if (saving || name.trim().length === 0) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);
    try {
      const result = await addToken(name.trim(), color);
      if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error.message);
      setName('');
      onClose();
    } catch (error) {
      setErrorMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal menambah token.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <BottomSheet open={open} title="Tambah Token" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xl font-bold text-white"
            style={{ backgroundColor: color, boxShadow: '0 0 0 3px rgba(15,23,42,0.9)' }}
          >
            {initial || '?'}
          </span>
          <label className="block flex-1 space-y-1">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Nama (inisial tampil di token)</span>
            <input
              value={name}
              maxLength={24}
              placeholder="mis. Goblin"
              onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
              className={inputClass}
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Warna</span>
          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {TOKEN_COLORS.map((preset) => (
              <button
                key={preset}
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Warna ${preset}`}
                aria-pressed={preset === color}
                onClick={() => setColor(preset)}
                className={`h-11 w-11 rounded-full ${
                  preset === color ? 'ring-2 ring-white ring-offset-2 ring-offset-slate-900' : ''
                }`}
                style={{ backgroundColor: preset }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={saving || name.trim().length === 0}
          onClick={handleAdd}
          className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl bg-indigo-500 text-sm font-medium text-white active:bg-indigo-400 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {saving ? 'Menambah…' : 'Tambah ke Peta'}
        </button>

        {errorMessage && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-400">
            {errorMessage}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}


--- FILE: client\src\features\token\TokenHPSheet.tsx ---
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { Token } from '@myvtt/shared';
import BottomSheet from '../../components/BottomSheet';
import { useRoomStore } from '../room/store';

interface TokenHPSheetProps {
  token: Token;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const clampHp = (value: number) => Math.min(999, Math.max(0, value));

/**
 * Bottom sheet HP token (C3): angka besar, tombol −/+ (>= 44 px, zona
 * jempol), dan input angka. Tiap perubahan dikirim `token:update` (DM);
 * pemain hanya melihat hingga kepemilikan token ada (C7/C8, izin A6).
 */
export default function TokenHPSheet({ token, onClose }: TokenHPSheetProps) {
  const role = useRoomStore((state) => state.role);
  const updateTokenHp = useRoomStore((state) => state.updateTokenHp);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(token.hp));
  const [lastHp, setLastHp] = useState(token.hp);
  const canEdit = role === 'dm';

  // Sinkron saat nilai server berubah (klien lain maupun rollback),
  // tanpa menimpa saat pengguna sedang mengetik (pola "adjust during render").
  if (token.hp !== lastHp) {
    setLastHp(token.hp);
    setDraft(String(token.hp));
  }

  function commitHp(hp: number) {
    setDraft(String(hp));
    void updateTokenHp(token.id, hp);
  }

  function commitDraft() {
    const parsed = Number(draft);
    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || draft.trim() === '') {
      setDraft(String(token.hp));
      return;
    }
    const hp = clampHp(Math.round(parsed));
    if (hp !== token.hp) commitHp(hp);
    else setDraft(String(hp));
  }

  return (
    <BottomSheet open title={`HP ${token.name}`} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          {canEdit ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label="Kurangi HP"
              disabled={token.hp <= 0}
              onClick={() => commitHp(clampHp(token.hp - 1))}
              className="h-14 w-14 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-100 active:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              −
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="h-14 w-14" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          <label className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <span className="sr-only">HP</span>
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              min={0}
              max={999}
              value={draft}
              readOnly={!canEdit}
              onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
              onBlur={commitDraft}
              className="min-h-14 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 text-center text-3xl font-bold text-slate-100 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </label>
          {canEdit ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label="Tambah HP"
              onClick={() => commitHp(clampHp(token.hp + 1))}
              className="h-14 w-14 rounded-xl bg-indigo-500 text-2xl font-bold text-white active:bg-indigo-400"
            >
              +
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="h-14 w-14" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
          {canEdit
            ? 'Ketuk −/+ atau ketik angka; perubahan langsung tersinkron.'
            : 'HP hanya dapat diubah oleh DM.'}
        </p>
      </div>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}


--- FILE: client\src\features\token\TokenLayer.tsx ---
import { useRef, useState, type PointerEvent } from 'react';
import type { GridConfig, Token } from '@myvtt/shared';
import { cellCenterToPixel, resolveGrid } from '../../lib/grid';
import { useRoomStore } from '../room/store';
import TokenView from './TokenView';

interface TokenLayerProps {
  tokens: Token[];
  grid: GridConfig | null;
  /** Skala tampilan viewport (layar→gambar) untuk mengubah delta jari. */
  scale: number;
  /** Dipanggil saat token diketuk tanpa digeser (C3: buka sheet HP). */
  onTapToken: (token: Token) => void;
}

interface DragState {
  id: string;
  pointerX: number;
  pointerY: number;
  originX: number;
  originY: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

/** Ambang layar (px) untuk membedakan ketuk vs geser (C3). */
const TAP_THRESHOLD_PX = 8;

const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

/**
 * Lapisan token + drag satu jari (C2). Jari menggerakkan token bebas
 * (posisi optimistik lokal), saat dilepas di-snap ke tengah kotak terdekat
 * lalu dikirim `token:move` (rollback di store bila server menolak).
 * Token menClaim gesture satu jari via stopPropagation — pan/zoom tetap
 * dua jari (B3). Semua koordinat dalam piksel gambar.
 */
export default function TokenLayer({ tokens, grid, scale, onTapToken }: TokenLayerProps) {
  const resolved = resolveGrid(grid);
  const role = useRoomStore((state) => state.role);
  const moveToken = useRoomStore((state) => state.moveToken);
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<DragState | null>(null);
  const dragRef = useRef<DragState | null>(null);
  // Izin peran (A6): sementara hanya DM; kepemilikan token pemain menyusul di C7/C8.
  const canDrag = role === 'dm';

  function startDrag(token: Token, event: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (!canDrag || scale <= 0) return;
    event.stopPropagation();
    event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
    const origin = cellCenterToPixel(token.col, token.row, resolved);
    const next: DragState = {
      id: token.id,
      pointerX: event.clientX,
      pointerY: event.clientY,
      originX: origin.x,
      originY: origin.y,
      x: origin.x,
      y: origin.y,
    };
    dragRef.current = next;
    setDrag(next);
  }

  function handleMove(event: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    const current = dragRef.current;
    if (!current || scale <= 0) return;
    const next: DragState = {
      ...current,
      x: current.originX + (event.clientX - current.pointerX) / scale,
      y: current.originY + (event.clientY - current.pointerY) / scale,
    };
    dragRef.current = next;
    setDrag(next);
  }

  function handleEnd(event: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    const current = dragRef.current;
    dragRef.current = null;
    setDrag(null);
    if (!current) return;

    // Ketuk (jarak jari di bawah ambang) = buka sheet HP; gerak = snap & kirim.
    const distance = Math.hypot(event.clientX - current.pointerX, event.clientY - current.pointerY);
    if (distance < TAP_THRESHOLD_PX) {
      const tapped = tokens.find((item) => item.id === current.id);
      if (tapped) onTapToken(tapped);
      return;
    }

    const col = clamp(Math.floor((current.x - resolved.offsetX) / resolved.cellPx), 0, 999);
    const row = clamp(Math.floor((current.y - resolved.offsetY) / resolved.cellPx), 0, 999);
    const token = tokens.find((item) => item.id === current.id);
    if (token && (token.col !== col || token.row !== row)) {
      void moveToken(current.id, col, row);
    }
  }

  const size = resolved.cellPx * 0.9;

  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0" aria-label="Token di peta">
      {tokens.map((token) => {
        const isDragging = drag?.id === token.id;
        const center =
          isDragging && drag
            ? { x: drag.x, y: drag.y }
            : cellCenterToPixel(token.col, token.row, resolved);
        return (
          <div
            key={token.id}
            className={`absolute touch-none ${
              canDrag ? 'cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing' : ''
            } ${isDragging ? 'z-10' : ''}`}
            style={{
              left: center.x - size / 2,
              top: center.y - size / 2,
              width: size,
              height: size,
            }}
            onPointerDown={(event) => startDrag(token, event)}
            onPointerMove={handleMove}
            onPointerUp={handleEnd}
            onPointerCancel={handleEnd}
          >
            <TokenView token={token} size={size} />
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}


--- FILE: client\src\features\token\TokenView.tsx ---
import type { Token } from '@myvtt/shared';

interface TokenViewProps {
  token: Token;
  /** Ukuran token dalam koordinat piksel gambar (mengisi elemen induk). */
  size: number;
}

/**
 * Token dasar (C1): warna + inisial; badge HP (C3) di tepi bawah.
 * Menjadi fallback otomatis saat token belum punya gambar atau gambar
 * gagal dimuat (C7 menyusul). Posisi/pemetaan drag dipegang induk.
 */
export default function TokenView({ token, size }: TokenViewProps) {
  return (
    <div
      role="img"
      aria-label={`${token.name}, HP ${token.hp}`}
      className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center rounded-full font-bold text-white select-none"
      style={{
        fontSize: size * 0.42,
        backgroundColor: token.color,
        boxShadow: `0 0 0 ${Math.max(1, size * 0.05)}px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.9)`,
      }}
    >
      {token.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
      <span
        className={`absolute -bottom-[12%] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900/95 px-[0.35em] font-semibold ${
          token.hp <= 0 ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-slate-100'
        }`}
        style={{ fontSize: size * 0.28, lineHeight: 1.4 }}
      >
        {token.hp}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FILE: client\src\lib\grid.ts ---
import { DEFAULT_GRID, type GridConfig } from '@myvtt/shared';

/** Grid yang dipakai render bila DM belum mengatur grid (B2). */
export function resolveGrid(grid: GridConfig | null): GridConfig {
  return grid ?? DEFAULT_GRID;
}

/**
 * (col,row) indeks kotak → pusat kotak dalam koordinat piksel gambar.
 * Konversi ke piksel hanya terjadi saat render (AGENTS.md §5).
 */
export function cellCenterToPixel(
  col: number,
  row: number,
  grid: GridConfig,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  return {
    x: grid.offsetX + (col + 0.5) * grid.cellPx,
    y: grid.offsetY + (row + 0.5) * grid.cellPx,
  };
}


--- FILE: client\src\lib\socket.ts ---
import { io, type Socket } from 'socket.io-client';

/** URL backend; kosong = memakai proxy Vite saat dev (lihat vite.config.ts). */
const SERVER_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_SERVER_URL ?? '';

let socket: Socket | null = null;

/**
 * Satu instance koneksi Socket.io untuk seluruh aplikasi (AGENTS.md §4).
 * Koneksi sengaja tidak dibuka otomatis: diaktifkan fitur A3 (sinkronisasi)
 * agar scaffold tidak bergantung pada server yang berjalan.
 */
export function getSocket(): Socket {
  if (!socket) {
    socket = io(SERVER_URL, { autoConnect: false });
  }
  return socket;
}

--- FILE: server\src\index.ts ---
import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import express from 'express';
import { Server } from 'socket.io';
import { registerHandlers } from './handlers';
import assetsRouter from './http/assetsRouter';

const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3001);
const CLIENT_ORIGIN = process.env.CLIENT_ORIGIN ?? 'http://localhost:5173';

const app = express();
app.use(express.json({ limit: '64kb' }));
app.get('/health', (_req, res) => {
  res.json({ ok: true, uptime: process.uptime() });
});
app.use(assetsRouter);

const httpServer = createServer(app);

// Server adalah sumber kebenaran; CORS dibatasi ke origin frontend (AGENTS.md §6).
const io = new Server(httpServer, {
  cors: { origin: CLIENT_ORIGIN, methods: ['GET', 'POST'] },
});

registerHandlers(io);

io.on('connection', (socket) => {
  console.log(`[socket] connected: ${socket.id}`);
  socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
    console.log(`[socket] disconnected: ${socket.id} (${reason})`);
  });
});

httpServer.listen(PORT, () => {
  console.log(`[server] listening on http://localhost:${PORT}`);
});


--- FILE: server\src\handlers\grid.ts ---
import type { Server } from 'socket.io';
import {
  CLIENT_EVENTS,
  SERVER_EVENTS,
  gridSetSchema,
  type ErrorPayload,
  type GridSetResult,
} from '@myvtt/shared';
import { getMembership, getRoom, getRoomState, setRoomGrid } from '../rooms/registry';

const INVALID_PAYLOAD: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload grid:set tidak valid',
};

/** Handler B2: DM mengatur grid peta; hasil disiarkan lewat state:full. */
export function registerGridHandlers(io: Server): void {
  io.on('connection', (socket) => {
    socket.on(CLIENT_EVENTS.GRID_SET, (payload: unknown, ack: (res: GridSetResult) => void) => {
      const parsed = gridSetSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_PAYLOAD);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

      const membership = getMembership(socket.id);
      if (!membership) {
        ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_IN_ROOM', message: 'Kamu belum masuk ruang.' } });
        return;
      }
      // Izin peran (A6): hanya DM yang boleh mengatur grid.
      if (membership.role !== 'dm') {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', message: 'Hanya DM yang boleh mengatur grid.' },
        });
        return;
      }
      if (!getRoom(membership.code)?.map) {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'NO_MAP', message: 'Unggah peta dulu sebelum mengatur grid.' },
        });
        return;
      }

      setRoomGrid(membership.code, parsed.data);
      console.log(
        `[grid] ${membership.code} cell=${parsed.data.cellPx}px locked=${parsed.data.locked}`,
      );

      const state = getRoomState(membership.code);
      if (state) io.to(membership.code).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.STATE_FULL, state);
      ack({ ok: true });
    });
  });
}


--- FILE: server\src\handlers\index.ts ---
import type { Server } from 'socket.io';
import { registerRoomHandlers } from './room';
import { registerMapHandlers } from './map';
import { registerGridHandlers } from './grid';
import { registerTokenHandlers } from './token';

/**
 * Pendaftaran seluruh handler event per grup (AGENTS.md §6).
 * Satu file per grup, sebagaimana direncanakan di struktur folder:
 * room.ts, map.ts, asset.ts, token.ts, fog.ts, dice.ts, initiative.ts, telemetry.ts.
 *
 * Setiap handler WAJIB memvalidasi payload dengan zod sebelum memutasi state
 * dan membalas event `error` (SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR) bila payload tidak valid.
 */
export function registerHandlers(io: Server): void {
  registerRoomHandlers(io);
  registerMapHandlers(io);
  registerGridHandlers(io);
  registerTokenHandlers(io);
}


--- FILE: server\src\handlers\map.ts ---
import type { Server } from 'socket.io';
import {
  CLIENT_EVENTS,
  SERVER_EVENTS,
  mapSetSchema,
  type ErrorPayload,
  type MapSetResult,
} from '@myvtt/shared';
import { getMembership, getRoomState, setRoomMap } from '../rooms/registry';

const INVALID_PAYLOAD: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload map:set tidak valid',
};

/** Handler B1: DM mengganti peta aktif ruang; hasil disiarkan lewat state:full. */
export function registerMapHandlers(io: Server): void {
  io.on('connection', (socket) => {
    socket.on(CLIENT_EVENTS.MAP_SET, (payload: unknown, ack: (res: MapSetResult) => void) => {
      const parsed = mapSetSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_PAYLOAD);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

      const membership = getMembership(socket.id);
      if (!membership) {
        ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_IN_ROOM', message: 'Kamu belum masuk ruang.' } });
        return;
      }
      // Izin peran (A6): hanya DM yang boleh mengganti peta.
      if (membership.role !== 'dm') {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', message: 'Hanya DM yang boleh mengganti peta.' },
        });
        return;
      }

      setRoomMap(membership.code, parsed.data);
      console.log(`[map] ${membership.code} updated by ${socket.id}`);

      const state = getRoomState(membership.code);
      if (state) io.to(membership.code).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.STATE_FULL, state);
      ack({ ok: true });
    });
  });
}


--- FILE: server\src\handlers\room.ts ---
import type { Server } from 'socket.io';
import {
  CLIENT_EVENTS,
  SERVER_EVENTS,
  roomCreateSchema,
  roomJoinSchema,
  type ErrorPayload,
  type RoomCreateAck,
  type RoomJoinResult,
} from '@myvtt/shared';
import {
  createRoom,
  getMembership,
  getRoom,
  getRoomState,
  joinRoom,
  leaveRoom,
} from '../rooms/registry';

const INVALID_CREATE_PAYLOAD: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload room:create tidak valid',
};

const INVALID_JOIN_PAYLOAD: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload room:join tidak valid',
};

/** Handler A1 (buat ruang) dan A2 (gabung ruang). */
export function registerRoomHandlers(io: Server): void {
  io.on('connection', (socket) => {
    socket.on(CLIENT_EVENTS.ROOM_CREATE, (payload: unknown, ack: (res: RoomCreateAck) => void) => {
      const parsed = roomCreateSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_CREATE_PAYLOAD);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

      const code = createRoom();
      console.log(`[room] created ${code} by ${socket.id}`);
      ack({ code });
    });

    socket.on(CLIENT_EVENTS.ROOM_JOIN, (payload: unknown, ack: (res: RoomJoinResult) => void) => {
      const parsed = roomJoinSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_JOIN_PAYLOAD);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

      const { code, nickname, role } = parsed.data;
      let outcome = joinRoom(socket.id, code, nickname, role);

      // A4: DM yang baru putus bisa jadi belum terproses server (disconnect baru
      // dipanggil setelah ping timeout). Slot DM boleh diambil alih oleh socket
      // dengan nama & peran sama; socket lama diputus paksa.
      if (outcome === 'DM_EXISTS' && role === 'dm') {
        const previousId = getRoom(code)?.dmSocketId ?? null;
        const previousSocket = previousId ? io.sockets.sockets.get(previousId) : undefined;
        if (previousId && previousSocket && getMembership(previousId)?.nickname === nickname) {
          previousSocket.disconnect(true);
          outcome = joinRoom(socket.id, code, nickname, role);
        }
      }

      if (outcome === 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND') {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND', message: 'Kode ruang tidak ditemukan.' },
        });
        return;
      }
      if (outcome === 'DM_EXISTS') {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'DM_EXISTS', message: 'Ruang ini sudah punya DM yang tersambung.' },
        });
        return;
      }

      console.log(`[room] ${socket.id} joined ${code} as ${role} (${nickname})`);
      ack({ ok: true, code, nickname, role });

      // A3: ruang = channel Socket.io; seluruh anggota (termasuk yang baru masuk)
      // menerima potret state terbaru dari server.
      socket.join(code);
      const state = getRoomState(code);
      if (state) io.to(code).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.STATE_FULL, state);
    });

    socket.on('disconnect', () => {
      const leftCode = leaveRoom(socket.id);
      if (!leftCode) return;
      const state = getRoomState(leftCode);
      if (state) io.to(leftCode).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.STATE_FULL, state);
    });
  });
}


--- FILE: server\src\handlers\token.ts ---
import type { Server } from 'socket.io';
import {
  CLIENT_EVENTS,
  SERVER_EVENTS,
  tokenAddSchema,
  tokenMoveSchema,
  tokenUpdateSchema,
  type ErrorPayload,
  type TokenAddResult,
  type TokenMoveResult,
  type TokenUpdateResult,
} from '@myvtt/shared';
import {
  addToken,
  getMembership,
  getRoom,
  getRoomState,
  moveToken,
  updateTokenHp,
} from '../rooms/registry';

const INVALID_PAYLOAD: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload token:add tidak valid',
};

const INVALID_MOVE: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload token:move tidak valid',
};

const INVALID_UPDATE: ErrorPayload = {
  code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD',
  message: 'Payload token:update tidak valid',
};

/** Handler C1: DM menambah token dasar (warna + inisial); disiarkan lewat state:full. */
export function registerTokenHandlers(io: Server): void {
  io.on('connection', (socket) => {
    socket.on(CLIENT_EVENTS.TOKEN_ADD, (payload: unknown, ack: (res: TokenAddResult) => void) => {
      const parsed = tokenAddSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_PAYLOAD);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

      const membership = getMembership(socket.id);
      if (!membership) {
        ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_IN_ROOM', message: 'Kamu belum masuk ruang.' } });
        return;
      }
      // Izin peran (A6): hanya DM yang boleh menambah token (pemetaan pemain menyusul di C7/C8).
      if (membership.role !== 'dm') {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', message: 'Hanya DM yang boleh menambah token.' },
        });
        return;
      }
      if (!getRoom(membership.code)?.map) {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'NO_MAP', message: 'Unggah peta dulu sebelum menambah token.' },
        });
        return;
      }

      const token = addToken(membership.code, parsed.data.name, parsed.data.color);
      if (!token) {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'NO_MAP', message: 'Unggah peta dulu sebelum menambah token.' },
        });
        return;
      }
      console.log(`[token] ${membership.code} +${token.name} @(${token.col},${token.row})`);

      const state = getRoomState(membership.code);
      if (state) io.to(membership.code).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.STATE_FULL, state);
      ack({ ok: true });
    });

    socket.on(CLIENT_EVENTS.TOKEN_MOVE, (payload: unknown, ack: (res: TokenMoveResult) => void) => {
      const parsed = tokenMoveSchema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_MOVE);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

      const membership = getMembership(socket.id);
      if (!membership) {
        ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_IN_ROOM', message: 'Kamu belum masuk ruang.' } });
        return;
      }
      // Izin peran (A6): sementara hanya DM; token milik pemain menyusul (C7/C8).
      if (membership.role !== 'dm') {
        ack({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', message: 'Hanya DM yang boleh menggerakkan token.' },
        });
        return;
      }

      const { id, col, row } = parsed.data;
      if (!moveToken(membership.code, id, col, row)) {
        ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'Token tidak ditemukan.' } });
        return;
      }

      // Disiarkan sebagai token:moved (bukan state:full) agar ringan saat drag sering terjadi.
      io.to(membership.code).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.TOKEN_MOVED, { id, col, row });
      ack({ ok: true });
    });

    socket.on(
      CLIENT_EVENTS.TOKEN_UPDATE,
      (payload: unknown, ack: (res: TokenUpdateResult) => void) => {
        const parsed = tokenUpdateSchema.safeParse(payload);
        if (!parsed.success) {
          socket.emit(SERVER_EVENTS.ERROR, INVALID_UPDATE);
          return;
        }
        if (typeof ack !== 'function') return;

        const membership = getMembership(socket.id);
        if (!membership) {
          ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_IN_ROOM', message: 'Kamu belum masuk ruang.' } });
          return;
        }
        // Izin peran (A6): sementara hanya DM; edit HP pemain menyusul (C7/C8).
        if (membership.role !== 'dm') {
          ack({
            ok: false,
            error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', message: 'Hanya DM yang boleh mengubah HP token.' },
          });
          return;
        }

        const { id, hp } = parsed.data;
        if (!updateTokenHp(membership.code, id, hp)) {
          ack({ ok: false, error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'Token tidak ditemukan.' } });
          return;
        }

        io.to(membership.code).emit(SERVER_EVENTS.TOKEN_UPDATED, { id, hp });
        ack({ ok: true });
      },
    );
  });
}


--- FILE: server\src\http\assetsRouter.ts ---
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { Router } from 'express';
import { assetSignatureRequestSchema } from '@myvtt/shared';
import { getRoom } from '../rooms/registry';

const CLIENT_ORIGIN = process.env.CLIENT_ORIGIN ?? 'http://localhost:5173';
const CLOUD_NAME = process.env.CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME ?? '';
const API_KEY = process.env.CLOUDINARY_API_KEY ?? '';
const API_SECRET = process.env.CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET ?? '';

const router = Router();

// Unggah berlangsung langsung dari klien ke Cloudinary (AGENTS.md §12);
// endpoint ini hanya memberi tanda tangan, secret tidak pernah menyentuh klien.
router.use((req, res, next) => {
  res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', CLIENT_ORIGIN);
  res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type');
  res.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'POST, OPTIONS');
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    res.sendStatus(204);
    return;
  }
  next();
});

router.post('/api/assets/signature', (req, res) => {
  if (!CLOUD_NAME || !API_KEY || !API_SECRET) {
    res.status(503).json({
      code: 'CLOUDINARY_NOT_CONFIGURED',
      message: 'Server belum dikonfigurasi untuk unggah aset (Cloudinary).',
    });
    return;
  }

  const parsed = assetSignatureRequestSchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res
      .status(400)
      .json({ code: 'INVALID_PAYLOAD', message: 'Permintaan tanda tangan tidak valid.' });
    return;
  }
  if (!getRoom(parsed.data.roomCode)) {
    res.status(404).json({ code: 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND', message: 'Kode ruang tidak ditemukan.' });
    return;
  }

  const timestamp = Math.round(Date.now() / 1000);
  // Folder per ruang (AGENTS.md §12): membatasi tempat aset disimpan.
  const folder = `myvtt/room-${parsed.data.roomCode}/${parsed.data.kind}s`;
  const params = { folder, format: 'webp', timestamp };
  const toSign = Object.keys(params)
    .sort()
    .map((key) => `${key}=${params[key as keyof typeof params]}`)
    .join('&');
  const signature = createHash('sha1')
    .update(toSign + API_SECRET)
    .digest('hex');

  res.json({
    cloudName: CLOUD_NAME,
    apiKey: API_KEY,
    uploadUrl: `https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUD_NAME}/image/upload`,
    timestamp,
    signature,
    folder,
  });
});

export default router;

--- FILE: server\src\rooms\registry.ts ---
import { randomInt, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import {
  DEFAULT_GRID,
  type GridConfig,
  type Role,
  type RoomFullState,
  type RoomMap,
  type RoomMember,
  type Token,
} from '@myvtt/shared';

// Huruf mirip (0/O, 1/I, L) dihilangkan agar kode mudah diketik ulang dari layar HP.
const CODE_ALPHABET = 'ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ23456789';
const CODE_LENGTH = 6;
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 20;
// HP awal token baru (C3); DM mengubahnya lewat bottom sheet setelah diletakkan.
const DEFAULT_TOKEN_HP = 10;

interface RoomRecord {
  createdAt: number;
  /** Socket pemegang peran DM; null bila DM belum masuk (A2). */
  dmSocketId: string | null;
  /** Peta aktif (B1); satu peta per ruang. */
  map: RoomMap | null;
  /** Parameter grid dalam koordinat gambar (B2). */
  grid: GridConfig | null;
  /** Daftar token dasar ruang (C1). */
  tokens: Token[];
}

interface Membership {
  code: string;
  nickname: string;
  role: Role;
}

// Penyimpanan sementara (A5 menambahkan persistensi MongoDB).
const rooms = new Map<string, RoomRecord>();
const memberships = new Map<string, Membership>();

/** Membuat ruang baru dan mengembalikan kode 6 karakter yang unik di server ini. */
export function createRoom(): string {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
    let code = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < CODE_LENGTH; i += 1) {
      code += CODE_ALPHABET.charAt(randomInt(CODE_ALPHABET.length));
    }
    if (!rooms.has(code)) {
      rooms.set(code, {
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        dmSocketId: null,
        map: null,
        grid: null,
        tokens: [],
      });
      return code;
    }
  }
  throw new Error('Gagal menghasilkan kode ruang unik');
}

export function getRoom(code: string): RoomRecord | undefined {
  return rooms.get(code);
}

export type JoinOutcome = 'ok' | 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND' | 'DM_EXISTS';

/**
 * Mencatat keanggotaan socket pada sebuah ruang (A2).
 * Peran DM hanya boleh satu socket tersambung agar izin A6 nanti tidak ambigu;
 * DM lama otomatis terlepas saat koneksi putus (disconnect).
 */
export function joinRoom(
  socketId: string,
  code: string,
  nickname: string,
  role: Role,
): JoinOutcome {
  const room = rooms.get(code);
  if (!room) return 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND';
  if (role === 'dm' && room.dmSocketId !== null && room.dmSocketId !== socketId) {
    return 'DM_EXISTS';
  }
  if (role === 'dm') room.dmSocketId = socketId;
  memberships.set(socketId, { code, nickname, role });
  return 'ok';
}

/**
 * Melepas keanggotaan socket saat koneksi terputus.
 * Mengembalikan kode ruang yang ditinggalkan (atau null) agar handler
 * dapat menyiarkan `state:full` terbaru ke ruang tersebut.
 */
export function leaveRoom(socketId: string): string | null {
  const membership = memberships.get(socketId);
  if (!membership) return null;
  memberships.delete(socketId);
  const room = rooms.get(membership.code);
  if (room && membership.role === 'dm' && room.dmSocketId === socketId) {
    room.dmSocketId = null;
  }
  return membership.code;
}

/** Potret state ruang untuk event `state:full` (A3). */
export function getRoomState(code: string): RoomFullState | null {
  const room = rooms.get(code);
  if (!room) return null;
  const members: RoomMember[] = [];
  for (const [socketId, membership] of memberships) {
    if (membership.code === code) {
      members.push({ socketId, nickname: membership.nickname, role: membership.role });
    }
  }
  return { code, members, map: room.map, grid: room.grid, tokens: room.tokens };
}

/** Mengganti peta aktif ruang (B1). */
export function setRoomMap(code: string, map: RoomMap): void {
  const room = rooms.get(code);
  if (room) room.map = map;
}

/** Mengganti parameter grid ruang (B2). */
export function setRoomGrid(code: string, grid: GridConfig): void {
  const room = rooms.get(code);
  if (room) room.grid = grid;
}

/**
 * Menambah token dasar (C1). Posisi spawn dihitung server dari grid
 * (atau grid bawaan) — mulai dari tengah peta, digeser agar tidak menumpuk.
 */
export function addToken(code: string, name: string, color: string): Token | null {
  const room = rooms.get(code);
  if (!room || !room.map) return null;
  const grid = room.grid ?? DEFAULT_GRID;
  const cols = Math.max(1, Math.floor((room.map.width - grid.offsetX) / grid.cellPx));
  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.floor((room.map.height - grid.offsetY) / grid.cellPx));
  const slot = Math.floor(rows / 2) * cols + Math.floor(cols / 2) + room.tokens.length;
  const token: Token = {
    id: randomUUID(),
    name,
    color,
    col: slot % cols,
    row: Math.min(rows - 1, Math.floor(slot / cols)),
    hp: DEFAULT_TOKEN_HP,
  };
  room.tokens.push(token);
  return token;
}

/** Memindahkan token (C2) ke indeks kotak baru; false bila id tidak ditemukan. */
export function moveToken(code: string, id: string, col: number, row: number): boolean {
  const token = rooms.get(code)?.tokens.find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!token) return false;
  token.col = col;
  token.row = row;
  return true;
}

/** Mengubah HP token (C3); false bila id tidak ditemukan. */
export function updateTokenHp(code: string, id: string, hp: number): boolean {
  const token = rooms.get(code)?.tokens.find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!token) return false;
  token.hp = hp;
  return true;
}

export function getMembership(
  socketId: string,
): { code: string; nickname: string; role: Role } | undefined {
  return memberships.get(socketId);
}

--- FILE: shared\src\events.ts ---
/**
 * Nama event Socket.io — satu-satunya sumber kebenaran (AGENTS.md §6).
 * Klien dan server hanya boleh memakai konstanta dari sini, bukan string literal.
 */

export const CLIENT_EVENTS = {
  ROOM_CREATE: 'room:create',
  ROOM_JOIN: 'room:join',
  MAP_SET: 'map:set',
  GRID_SET: 'grid:set',
  ASSET_ADD: 'asset:add',
  ASSET_REMOVE: 'asset:remove',
  TOKEN_ADD: 'token:add',
  TOKEN_MOVE: 'token:move',
  TOKEN_UPDATE: 'token:update',
  TOKEN_REMOVE: 'token:remove',
  FOG_SET: 'fog:set',
  DICE_ROLL: 'dice:roll',
  INITIATIVE_UPDATE: 'initiative:update',
  UNDO_REQUEST: 'undo:request',
  TELEMETRY_EVENT: 'telemetry:event',
} as const;

export const SERVER_EVENTS = {
  STATE_FULL: 'state:full',
  ASSET_ADDED: 'asset:added',
  TOKEN_MOVED: 'token:moved',
  TOKEN_UPDATED: 'token:updated',
  FOG_UPDATED: 'fog:updated',
  LOG_APPENDED: 'log:appended',
  INITIATIVE_UPDATED: 'initiative:updated',
  ERROR: 'error',
} as const;

export type ClientEvent = (typeof CLIENT_EVENTS)[keyof typeof CLIENT_EVENTS];
export type ServerEvent = (typeof SERVER_EVENTS)[keyof typeof SERVER_EVENTS];

--- FILE: shared\src\schemas.ts ---
import { z } from 'zod';
import { TOKEN_COLORS } from './types';

/**
 * Skema validasi zod per event (AGENTS.md §5 dan §6).
 * Setiap handler server memvalidasi payload memakai skema di sini sebelum
 * memutasi state, dan membalas event `error` bila tidak valid.
 */

/** Payload `room:create` (A1): DM membuat ruang, server menghasilkan kode. */
export const roomCreateSchema = z.object({});

export type RoomCreatePayload = z.infer<typeof roomCreateSchema>;

/** Payload `room:join` (A2): masuk ruang dengan kode + nama panggilan + peran. */
export const roomJoinSchema = z.object({
  // Kode harus enam karakter alfabet/angka (huruf mirip 0/O/1/I tidak dipakai).
  code: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase()
    .regex(/^[A-HJ-NP-TV-Z2-9]{6}$/),
  nickname: z.string().trim().min(1).max(24),
  role: z.enum(['dm', 'player']),
});

export type RoomJoinPayload = z.infer<typeof roomJoinSchema>;

/**
 * Payload `map:set` (B1): hasil unggah peta yang sudah dikompres di klien.
 * URL wajib Cloudinary agar state bersama hanya berisi aset CDN tepercaya.
 */
export const mapSetSchema = z.object({
  url: z
    .url()
    .max(512)
    .regex(/^https:\/\/res\.cloudinary\.com\//),
  width: z.number().int().min(1).max(4096),
  height: z.number().int().min(1).max(4096),
});

export type MapSetPayload = z.infer<typeof mapSetSchema>;

/** Permintaan tanda tangan unggah Cloudinary (B1, REST `/api/assets/signature`). */
export const assetSignatureRequestSchema = z.object({
  roomCode: z.string().regex(/^[A-HJ-NP-TV-Z2-9]{6}$/),
  kind: z.enum(['map']),
});

export type AssetSignatureRequest = z.infer<typeof assetSignatureRequestSchema>;

/**
 * Payload `grid:set` (B2): parameter grid dalam koordinat piksel gambar
 * peta (bukan piksel layar) agar tidak bergeser saat zoom atau resize.
 */
export const gridSetSchema = z.object({
  cellPx: z.number().int().min(8).max(4096),
  offsetX: z.number().int().min(0).max(4096),
  offsetY: z.number().int().min(0).max(4096),
  locked: z.boolean(),
  color: z.string().regex(/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/),
  opacity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
});

export type GridSetPayload = z.infer<typeof gridSetSchema>;

/**
 * Payload `token:add` (C1): DM menambah token dasar (warna + inisial).
 * Posisi (col,row) dihitung server agar spawn tidak menumpuk.
 */
export const tokenAddSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(24),
  color: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/)
    .refine((value) => (TOKEN_COLORS as readonly string[]).includes(value), {
      message: 'warna harus salah satu dari preset',
    }),
});

export type TokenAddPayload = z.infer<typeof tokenAddSchema>;

/**
 * Payload `token:move` (C2): hasil snap-to-grid setelah token dilepas.
 * `id` adalah UUID yang dibuat server (C1); col/row indeks kotak hasil snap.
 */
export const tokenMoveSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i),
  col: z.number().int().min(0).max(999),
  row: z.number().int().min(0).max(999),
});

export type TokenMovePayload = z.infer<typeof tokenMoveSchema>;

/** Payload `token:update` (C3): ubah HP token (ketuk −/+ atau ketik angka). */
export const tokenUpdateSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i),
  hp: z.number().int().min(0).max(999),
});

export type TokenUpdatePayload = z.infer<typeof tokenUpdateSchema>;


--- FILE: shared\src\types.ts ---
/** Tipe domain bersama klien dan server (AGENTS.md §5). */

export type Role = 'dm' | 'player';

/** Ukuran token dalam satuan kotak grid: Medium 1, Large 2, Huge 3. */
export type TokenSize = 1 | 2 | 3;

/**
 * Parameter grid dalam koordinat piksel gambar peta (bukan piksel layar),
 * agar tidak bergeser saat zoom atau resize (AGENTS.md §12).
 */
export interface GridConfig {
  cellPx: number;
  offsetX: number;
  offsetY: number;
  locked: boolean;
  color: string;
  opacity: number;
}

/** Warna preset token (C1): dipilih klien, divalidasi server lewat skema. */
export const TOKEN_COLORS = [
  '#ef4444',
  '#f97316',
  '#eab308',
  '#22c55e',
  '#06b6d4',
  '#6366f1',
  '#a855f7',
  '#ec4899',
] as const;

/** Grid bawaan sebelum DM mengatur grid (B2); dipakai hitungan server & render. */
export const DEFAULT_GRID: GridConfig = {
  cellPx: 70,
  offsetX: 0,
  offsetY: 0,
  locked: false,
  color: '#3b82f6',
  opacity: 0.6,
};

/**
 * Token dasar (C1): warna + inisial bawaan (bila belum/gagal punya gambar).
 * Posisi disimpan sebagai indeks kotak grid (col,row), bukan piksel (AGENTS.md §5).
 */
export interface Token {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  col: number;
  row: number;
  color: string;
  /** HP token (C3); default diisi server, diubah lewat `token:update`. */
  hp: number;
}

/** Payload bawaan event server→klien `error`. */
export interface ErrorPayload {
  code: string;
  message: string;
}

/**
 * Hasil acknowledgment event `room:create` (A1).
 * Kode 6 karakter unik di server, dibagikan ke pemain lewat fitur A2.
 */
export interface RoomCreateAck {
  code: string;
}

/**
 * Hasil acknowledgment event `room:join` (A2).
 * Kegagalan domain (kode salah, DM sudah ada) memakai `ok: false` agar
 * bisa ditampilkan langsung ke pengguna.
 */
export type RoomJoinResult =
  { ok: true; code: string; nickname: string; role: Role } | { ok: false; error: ErrorPayload };

export interface RoomMember {
  socketId: string;
  nickname: string;
  role: Role;
}

/**
 * Payload event `state:full` (A3): potret state ruang dari server,
 * satu-satunya sumber kebenaran klien. Bidang baru (token, fog, log)
 * ditambahkan bersama fiturnya.
 */
export interface RoomFullState {
  code: string;
  members: RoomMember[];
  map: RoomMap | null;
  grid: GridConfig | null;
  tokens: Token[];
}

/** Peta aktif ruang (B1); dimensi dalam piksel gambar hasil kompresi. */
export interface RoomMap {
  url: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** Hasil acknowledgment event `map:set` (B1). */
export type MapSetResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: ErrorPayload };

/** Hasil acknowledgment event `grid:set` (B2). */
export type GridSetResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: ErrorPayload };

/** Hasil acknowledgment event `token:add` (C1). */
export type TokenAddResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: ErrorPayload };

/** Payload event server→klien `token:moved` (C2): posisi token tersinkron. */
export interface TokenMovedPayload {
  id: string;
  col: number;
  row: number;
}

/** Hasil acknowledgment event `token:move` (C2). */
export type TokenMoveResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: ErrorPayload };

/** Payload event server→klien `token:updated` (C3): HP token tersinkron. */
export interface TokenUpdatedPayload {
  id: string;
  hp: number;
}

/** Hasil acknowledgment event `token:update` (C3). */
export type TokenUpdateResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: ErrorPayload };
